# [Issue #11048] GPU RDMA not working with Broadcom RoCE connected via PHB

source: https://github.com/openucx/ucx/issues/11048
state: open | updated: 2026-01-05T14:18:20Z
labels: 

## 正文

Hello,

I am using a RoCEv2 from Broadcom and I am running into an error when I try to use the device.

## Error
I am running `ucx_perftest` using host memory and forcing the usage of the RoCEv2 device.
```bash
UCX_LOG_LEVEL=trace UCX_TLS=rc ucx_perftest $IB_HOST -t ucp_am_bw -m host -s 10M
```
Output: see comment below.


## Debug commands
I run all debug commands I was able to find. To me it seems that the network interfaces are fine. ACS is disabled. Also `ib_write_bw` works as well.
```bash

### hahahannes · 2025-12-10

Using `rping` on both nodes seems fine as well
```
rping -s -a $(hostname) -v -C 4
rping -c -I $(hostname) -a $HOST -v -C 4
```

```
server ping data: rdma-ping-0: ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ[\]^_`abcdefghijklmnopqr
server ping data: rdma-ping-1: BCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ[\]^_`abcdefghijklmnopqrs
server ping data: rdma-ping-2: CDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ[\]^_`abcdefghijklmnopqrst
server ping data: rdma-ping-3: DEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ[\]^_`abcdefghijklmnopqrstu
server DISCONNECT EVENT...
wait for RDMA_READ_ADV state 10
```

UCX_IB_ROCE_REACHABILITY_MODE=all UCX_LOG_LEVEL=trace UCX_TLS=rc ucx_perftest $IB_HOST -t ucp_am_bw -m host -s 10M

### hahahannes · 2025-12-11

I noticed that the `bnxt_re0` entry was missing in the UCP endpoints:
```
ucx_info -u r -e
#
# UCP endpoint 
#
#               peer: <no debug data>
#                 lane[0]:  0:self/memory.0 md[0]           -> md[0]/self/sysdev[255] seg 4294967295 am rkey_ptr
#                 lane[1]: 12:cma/memory.0 md[8]            -> md[8]/cma/sysdev[255] seg 4294967295 rma_bw#0
#                 lane[2]:  7:cuda_copy/cuda.0 md[4]        -> md[4]/cuda_cpy/sysdev[255] seg 4294967295 rma_bw#1
``` 

I was able to fix that by installing the latest `libibverbs` package (v57) and upgrading ucx to v1.19.1.
Now `UCX_IB_ROCE_REACHABILITY_MODE=all UCX_LOG_LEVEL=trace UCX_TLS=rc ucx_perftest $IB_HOST -t ucp_am_bw -m host -s 10M` is working.


But for some reason, the device is only recognized for host memory (` memory types: host (access,reg,cache)`):
`ucx_info -d`:
```
Memory domain: bnxt_re0
#     Component: ib
#             register: unlimited, dmabuf, cost: 16000 + 0.060 * N nsec

#           local memory handle is required for zcopy

#

#            bandwidth: 10957.84/ppn + 0.00 MB/sec

### hahahannes · 2025-12-12

Though, one GPU is on the same root complex as the RoCEv2 NIC. Therefore I would assume that GPU RDMA should work when this GPU is used.

### hahahannes · 2026-01-05

Hello everyone,

to quickly summarize:
- Host-to-Host communication worked after upgrading `libibverbs` and `UCX`
- GPUDirect RDMA works for GPUs that are connected within the same NUMA node to the NIC (`NODE` in `nvidia-smi topo -m`) 
- GPUDirect RDMA works for one GPU that is connected within the same PCIe root complec to the NIC (`PHB` in `nvidia-smi topo -m`). This is a bit surprising though

To test whether GPUDirect RDMA works/doesn't work, I use a simple MPI script (compiled with CUDA and UCX) and check via `nsys` if any `cuMemcpy` calls are made. Strangely `ib_write_bw -d bnxt_re0 --use_cuda=GPU_IDX -a -x 3 --report_gbits -F --use_cuda_dmabuf --ipv6 -v $IB_HOST` returns the expected bandwith for all GPUs.
